# SutraERP L6 — Workflow Realization Register — Master Reconciled

**Version:** 1.0.0
**Status:** MASTER-RECONCILED / STAGE-2 CANONICAL REALIZATION REGISTER
**Owner:** Lead Architect
**Architecture Level:** L6 — UI / UX Architecture Support
**Last Updated:** 2026-09-21
**Depends On:** canonical 1,084-scenario L3 corpus; validated Stage-2 Groups 1–14; `03_Workflow_Realization_Audit/group-reconciliation/STAGE2_GROUP_15_MASTER_CROSS_GROUP_RECONCILIATION.md`.
**Purpose:** Record exactly how every canonical L3 scenario is realized, observed or deliberately not directly interacted with in the SutraERP UI without duplicating business architecture.

> This register is a realization/coverage locator. It is not a new business-workflow authority, authorization model, backend contract, or requirement for one screen per scenario.

## 1. Coverage Rule

```text
Canonical L3 scenarios          1,084
Realization rows                1,084
Unique primary assignments      1,084
Unexplained scenarios               0
Final normalized workflows        595
```

## 2. Final L6 Workflow Identity

`L6-RWF-####` is an internal L6 realization-workflow identity created by the Stage-2 master reconciliation. It is reviewer/traceability metadata only and must not appear in ordinary end-user UX.

No cross-group workflow merge was valid because no L6 workspace occurs in more than one group and workflow identity cannot cross canonical workspace/owner boundaries.

## 3. Primary Dispositions

Allowed values:

- `INTERACTIVE-WORKFLOW`
- `REVIEW-APPROVAL-WORKFLOW`
- `TRACKING-WORKFLOW`
- `EXCEPTION-RECOVERY-WORKFLOW`
- `CROSS-WORKSPACE-HANDOFF`
- `SYSTEM-BACKGROUND-OBSERVABLE`
- `NO-DIRECT-END-USER-INTERACTION`

Group 15 uses the final normalized workflow disposition as each scenario's primary L6 disposition, except where a validated group explicitly documented an automatic/non-mock sub-outcome inside an otherwise interactive workflow. Those scenario-specific overrides preserve the prohibition on fake UI actions.

## 5. Required Fields

Each row records scenario identity/title, workspace, final normalized workflow, presentation profile, primary disposition, discovery route, observable outcome, mock requirement, UX archetypes, owner/handoff, upstream trace and final maturity.
